# Card 066 proof (a): late join after a compaction. The host plays alone (the client's travel waits
# UBWCOOP_TRAVEL_DELAY_MS=360000): Morita pickup, the shooting range route of card 065 with every target shot by the
# host (a reload between kills), then the bug nest with the frag. With UBWCOOP_C66_CAP=64 the journal compacts at
# least once before the client joins; the client's first Pong then gets the compacted journal, and the `objectives`
# dumps of both sides must list the same objectives with equal counts. Generated from c65-range.txt by gen66.py.

#      CLIENT_ENV="UBWCOOP_TRAVEL_DELAY_MS=360000" run65.sh <tag> 900
